Method: 研究采用了神经符号推理框架，结合大型语言模型（LLM）、符号计算工具（计算机代数、约束求解器、模拟退火）以及人类战略指导。通过详细交互日志重建发现过程，分析各组件的认知贡献。形式化验证在Lean 4中完成。

Result: 得到拉丁方不平衡性的紧凑下界：4n(n-1)/9（当n≡1(mod 3)时）。该界通过一类新的近完美置换实现，并已在Lean 4中形式化验证。

Conclusion: 多模态大语言模型 deliberation在前沿模型中对于批评和错误检测是可靠的，但对于建设性主张是不可靠的。神经符号系统确实能够在纯数学中产生真正的发现。

Abstract: We study mathematical discovery through the lens of neurosymbolic reasoning, where an AI agent powered by a large language model (LLM), coupled with symbolic computation tools, and human strategic direction, jointly produced a new result in combinatorial design theory. The main result of this human-AI collaboration is a tight lower bound on the imbalance of Latin squares for the notoriously difficult case $n \equiv 1 \pmod{3}$.
  We reconstruct the discovery process from detailed interaction logs spanning multiple sessions over several days and identify the distinct cognitive contributions of each component. The AI agent proved effective at uncovering hidden structure and generating hypotheses. The symbolic component consists of computer algebra, constraint solvers, and simulated annealing, which provides rigorous verification and exhaustive enumeration. Human steering supplied the critical research pivot that transformed a dead end into a productive inquiry. Our analysis reveals that multi-model deliberation among frontier LLMs proved reliable for criticism and error detection but unreliable for constructive claims.
  The resulting human-AI mathematical contribution, a tight lower bound of $4n(n{-}1)/9$, is achieved via a novel class of near-perfect permutations. The bound was formally verified in Lean 4. Our experiments show that neurosymbolic systems can indeed produce genuine discoveries in pure mathematics.

</details>


### [248] [M$^3$-ACE: Rectifying Visual Perception in Multimodal Math Reasoning via Multi-Agentic Context Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08369)
*Peijin Xie,Zhen Xu,Bingquan Liu,Baoxun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对多模态大语言模型在视觉数学推理中因视觉感知不准确导致性能受限的问题，提出M3-ACE多智能体框架，通过解耦感知与推理、动态维护共享视觉证据上下文、Summary Tool和Refine Tool实现协作修正，在MathVision上达到89.1 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型在视觉数学推理中性能受限的根本原因是视觉感知不准确或不完整，而非推理能力不足；且模型对初始感知过度自信，传统方法（提示工程、多轮自我反思、后验引导）难以可靠修正错误。

Method: 提出M3-ACE框架，采用多智能体上下文工程方法：1) 将感知与推理解耦；2) 通过动态维护以视觉证据列表为中心的共享上下文实现多智能体协作；3) 引入Summary Tool将不同智能体的证据组织为一致、互补和冲突组件；4) 引入Refine Tool过滤不可靠样本并指导迭代修正。

Result: 在MathVision基准上达到89.1的SOTA性能；在MathVista和MathVerse等其他相关数据集上获得一致提升。

Conclusion: M3-ACE通过多智能体协作框架有效解决了多模态大语言模型在视觉数学推理中的感知瓶颈问题。实验表明该方法在MathVision上达到89.1的SOTA性能，并在MathVista和MathVerse上获得一致提升，证明了以感知为中心的多智能体协作对推进多模态推理系统的重要性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have recently shown promising progress in visual mathematical reasoning. However, their performance is often limited by a critical yet underexplored bottleneck: inaccurate visual perception. Through systematic analysis, we find that the most failures originate from incorrect or incomplete visual evidence extraction rather than deficiencies in reasoning capability. Moreover, models tend to remain overly confident in their initial perceptions, making standard strategies such as prompt engineering, multi-round self-reflection, or posterior guidance insufficient to reliably correct errors.
  To address this limitation, we propose M3-ACE, a multi-agentic context engineering framework designed to rectify visual perception in multimodal math reasoning. Instead of directly aggregating final answers, our approach decouples perception and reasoning by dynamically maintaining a shared context centered on visual evidence lists. Multiple agents collaboratively contribute complementary observations, enabling the system to expose inconsistencies and recover missing perceptual information. To support stable multi-turn collaboration, we further introduce two lightweight tools: a Summary Tool that organizes evidence from different agents into consistent, complementary, and conflicting components, and a Refine Tool that filters unreliable samples and guides iterative correction.
  Extensive experiments demonstrate that M3-ACE substantially improves visual mathematical reasoning performance across multiple benchmarks. Our method establishes new state-of-the-art results 89.1 on the MathVision benchmark and achieves consistent improvements on other related datasets, including MathVista and MathVerse. These results highlight the importance of perception-centric multi-agent collaboration for advancing multimodal reasoning systems.

</details>


### [249] [A Hierarchical Error-Corrective Graph Framework for Autonomous Agents with LLM-Based Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08388)
*Cong Cao,Jingyao Zhang,Kun Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HECG是一个用于LLM驱动自主智能体的层级错误纠正图框架，通过多维度可迁移策略(MDTS)、错误矩阵分类(EMC)和因果上下文图检索(CCGR)三项核心创新，分别解决策略选择负迁移、错误归因粗糙和上下文检索不足的问题，显著提升复杂多步任务的执行可靠性。




<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的自主智能体面临三大挑战：(1) 策略选择仅依赖单一性能指标，难以避免负迁移；(2) 错误分析缺乏结构化归因手段，仅有整体成功率等粗粒度指标；(3) 动态任务环境中缺乏有效的上下文检索机制来加速策略适配。这些问题导致复杂多步任务执行可靠性不足。



Method: 提出三层创新方法：(1) MDTS整合任务质量指标(Q)、置信度/成本指标(C)、奖励指标(R)及LLM语义推理分数(LLM-Score)进行多维度策略对齐；(2) EMC将错误分类为10种类型并按严重程度、可恢复性等维度分解；(3) CCGR从历史状态-动作-事件序列构建因果图，通过子图检索匹配当前任务上下文。



Result: 实现了更精准的高质量候选策略选择，有效降低负迁移风险；提供任务失败根因的精细化分析，明确指导错误修正与策略优化；通过图结构捕获超越向量相似性的语义关系，充分挖掘上下文信息以加速策略适应。



Conclusion: HECG框架通过三层创新显著提升了LLM驱动的自主智能体在复杂多步任务中的执行可靠性。MDTS通过多维度策略选择降低负迁移风险；EMC通过精细化错误分类提供根因分析；CCGR通过图结构检索增强上下文理解能力。三者协同实现从策略选择、错误诊断到上下文利用的完整优化闭环。



Abstract: We propose a Hierarchical Error-Corrective Graph FrameworkforAutonomousAgentswithLLM-BasedActionGeneration(HECG),whichincorporates three core innovations: (1) Multi-Dimensional Transferable Strategy (MDTS): by integrating task quality metrics (Q), confidence/cost metrics (C), reward metrics (R), and LLM-based semantic reasoning scores (LLM-Score), MDTS achieves multi-dimensional alignment between quantitative performance and semantic context, enabling more precise selection of high-quality candidate strate gies and effectively reducing the risk of negative transfer. (2) Error Matrix Classification (EMC): unlike simple confusion matrices or overall performance metrics, EMC provides structured attribution of task failures by categorizing errors into ten types, such as Strategy Errors (Strategy Whe) and Script Parsing Errors (Script-Parsing-Error), and decomposing them according to severity, typical actions, error descriptions, and recoverability. This allows precise analysis of the root causes of task failures, offering clear guidance for subsequent error correction and strategy optimization rather than relying solely on overall success rates or single performance metrics. (3) Causal-Context Graph Retrieval (CCGR): to enhance agent retrieval capabilities in dynamic task environments, we construct graphs from historical states, actions, and event sequences, where nodes store executed actions, next-step actions, execution states, transferable strategies, and other relevant information, and edges represent causal dependencies such as preconditions for transitions between nodes. CCGR identifies subgraphs most relevant to the current task context, effectively capturing structural relationships beyond vector similarity, allowing agents to fully leverage contextual information, accelerate strategy adaptation, and improve execution reliability in complex, multi-step tasks.

</details>


### [250] [Efficient Policy Learning with Hybrid Evaluation-Based Genetic Programming for Uncertain Agile Earth Observation Satellite Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08447)
*Junhua Xue,Yuning Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对不确定敏捷地球观测卫星调度问题(UAEOSSP)，提出了一种混合评估遗传编程方法(HE-GP)。该方法通过动态切换精确评估和近似评估模式，在保证调度性能的同时显著降低了计算成本。实验表明，HE-GP在16个实例集上表现优于手工启发式和单评估GPHH，训练时间平均减少17.77%，并在所有场景中获得最高平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1) UAEOSSP是符合当前航天技术发展需求的组合优化问题和实际工程挑战；2) 利润、资源消耗和可见性的不确定性可能使预计划调度方案次优甚至不可行；3) 遗传编程超启发式(GPHH)在演化可解释调度策略方面有前景，但其基于仿真的评估产生高计算成本；4) 在线调度算法(OSA)的设计直接影响适应度评估，导致策略空间中出现评估依赖的局部最优。

Method: 提出混合评估遗传编程(HE-GP)方法，将混合评估(HE)机制集成到策略驱动的OSA中：1) 精确模式：通过精心设计的约束验证模块确保评估准确性；2) 近似模式：通过简化逻辑减少计算开销；3) HE-GP根据实时进化状态信息动态切换评估模型。

Result: 在16个模拟实例集上的实验表明：1) HE-GP显著优于手工启发式和基于单评估的GPHH；2) 在保持优异调度性能的同时大幅降低计算成本；3) 平均训练时间比纯精确评估的GP减少17.77%；4) 生成的最优策略在所有场景中获得最高平均排名。

Conclusion: HE-GP通过动态平衡评估准确性和计算效率，有效解决了UAEOSSP问题。该方法为不确定环境下的卫星调度提供了一种有前景的演化学习范式，具有重要的理论意义和工程应用价值。

Abstract: The Uncertain Agile Earth Observation Satellite Scheduling Problem (UAEOSSP) is a novel combinatorial optimization problem and a practical engineering challenge that aligns with the current demands of space technology development. It incorporates uncertainties in profit, resource consumption, and visibility, which may render pre-planned schedules suboptimal or even infeasible. Genetic Programming Hyper-Heuristic (GPHH) shows promise for evolving interpretable scheduling policies; however, their simulation-based evaluation incurs high computational costs. Moreover, the design of the constructive method, denoted as Online Scheduling Algorithm (OSA), directly affects fitness assessment, resulting in evaluation-dependent local optima within the policy space. To address these issues, this paper proposes a Hybrid Evaluation-based Genetic Programming (HE-GP) for effectively solving UAEOSSP. A Hybrid Evaluation (HE) mechanism is integrated into the policy-driven OSA, combining exact and approximate filtering modes: exact mode ensures evaluation accuracy through elaborately designed constraint verification modules, while approximate mode reduces computational overhead via simplified logic. HE-GP dynamically switches between evaluation models based on real-time evolutionary state information. Experiments on 16 simulated instance sets demonstrate that HE-GP significantly outperforms handcrafted heuristics and single-evaluation based GPHH, achieving substantial reductions in computational cost while maintaining excellent scheduling performance across diverse scenarios. Specifically, the average training time of HE-GP was reduced by 17.77\% compared to GP employing exclusively exact evaluation, while the optimal policy generated by HE-GP achieved the highest average ranks across all scenarios.

</details>


### [251] [RetroAgent: From Solving to Evolving via Retrospective Dual Intrinsic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08561)
*Xiaoying Zhang,Zichen Liu,Yipeng Zhang,Xia Hu,Wenqi Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RetroAgent是一个基于在线强化学习的框架，通过后见自我反思机制产生双重内在反馈（数值反馈和语言反馈），并使用SimUtil-UCB策略检索过去经验，使LLM智能体能够在复杂交互环境中持续学习和适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RL范式存在两个关键局限：1) 因探索不足导致智能体收敛到次优策略；2) 学到的知识隐式存储在参数中而非显式可检索，限制了有效的经验学习。现有方法在复杂交互任务中表现不佳，需要一种能够持续适应并显式利用过去经验的框架。

Method: RetroAgent框架包含三个核心组件：1) 后见自我反思机制，产生双重内在反馈——内在数值反馈追踪增量子任务完成度以奖励有前景的探索，内在语言反馈将可复用教训提炼到记忆缓冲区；2) SimUtil-UCB（相似度与效用感知上置信界）策略，通过平衡相关性、效用和探索来检索过去经验；3) 在线RL训练范式，使智能体能够持续进化而非静态收敛。

Result: 在两个模型家族、四个挑战性智能体任务上的广泛实验表明，RetroAgent显著优于现有方法，取得最先进结果：相比GRPO训练的智能体，在ALFWorld上提升+18.3%，WebShop上提升+15.4%，Sokoban上提升+27.1%，MineSweeper上提升+8.9%。同时展现出强大的测试时适应和分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: RetroAgent通过引入后见自我反思机制和双重内在反馈有效解决了标准RL范式的局限性，显著提升了LLM智能体在复杂交互任务中的表现。该工作为增强智能体的持续适应能力和显式知识利用提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents trained with reinforcement learning (RL) have shown strong potential on complex interactive tasks. However, standard RL paradigms favor static problem-solving over continuous adaptation: agents often converge to suboptimal strategies due to insufficient exploration, while learned knowledge remains implicit within parameters rather than explicitly retrievable, limiting effective experiential learning. To address these limitations, we introduce RetroAgent, an online RL framework that empowers agents to master complex interactive environments not just by solving, but by evolving. Concretely, RetroAgent features a hindsight self-reflection mechanism that produces dual intrinsic feedback: (1) intrinsic numerical feedback that that tracks incremental subtask completion relative to prior attempts, rewarding promising explorations, and (2) intrinsic language feedback that distills reusable lessons into a memory buffer, retrieved via our proposed Similarity & Utility-Aware Upper Confidence Bound (SimUtil-UCB) strategy balancing relevance, utility, and exploration to effectively leverage past experiences. Extensive experiments on two model families across four challenging agentic tasks demonstrate that RetroAgent significantly outperforms existing methods, achieving state-of-the-art results -- e.g., surpassing Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)-trained agents by +18.3% on ALFWorld, +15.4% on WebShop, +27.1% on Sokoban, and +8.9% on MineSweeper -- while exhibiting strong test-time adaptation and generalization to out-of-distribution scenarios.

</details>


### [252] [Trust via Reputation of Conviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08575)
*Aravind R. Iyengar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于"信念"(conviction)的信任数学框架，将真理定义为可重复感知的知识子集，将来源形式化为具有生成和判别双重角色，将声誉定义为预期加权符号信念。论证信念而非正确性或忠诚度是信任的原则性基础，连续验证是声誉积累的理论必要性和实践机制，并应用于AI智能体的信任问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决知识、真理和信任的基础问题，特别是在AI智能体成为错误-prone来源的背景下，寻求一种与制度无关、奖励真正贡献的信任原则性基础。

Method: 1) 将知识定义为命题和来源的数学形式化；2) 将真理定义为知识中可重复感知的子集；3) 将来源形式化为同时具有生成性和判别性角色；4) 将声誉定义为领域上预期加权符号信念；5) 表征来源-命题制度下的行为；6) 识别连续验证作为声誉积累机制

Result: 1) 信念(而非正确性或忠诚度)是信任的原则性基础；2) 信念具有制度独立性、奖励真正贡献、要求透明自足的感知等特性；3) 连续验证是理论必要性和实践机制；4) 对于AI智能体，可验证的信念和持续积累的声誉构成稳健的信任基础

Conclusion: 该研究建立了基于"信念"(conviction)的信任数学框架，论证了信念而非正确性或忠诚度是信任的 principled 基础。连续验证既是理论必要性也是声誉积累的实践机制。对于AI智能体而言，可验证的信念和持续积累的声誉构成了稳健的信任基础。

Abstract: The question of \emph{knowledge}, \emph{truth} and \emph{trust} is explored via a mathematical formulation of claims and sources. We define truth as the reproducibly perceived subset of knowledge, formalize sources as having both generative and discriminative roles, and develop a framework for reputation grounded in the \emph{conviction} -- the likelihood that a source's stance is vindicated by independent consensus. We argue that conviction, rather than correctness or faithfulness, is the principled basis for trust: it is regime-independent, rewards genuine contribution, and demands the transparent and self-sufficient perceptions that make external verification possible. We formalize reputation as the expected weighted signed conviction over a realm of claims, characterize its behavior across source-claim regimes, and identify continuous verification as both a theoretical necessity and a practical mechanism through which reputation accrues. The framework is applied to AI agents, which are identified as capable but error-prone sources for whom verifiable conviction and continuously accrued reputation constitute the only robust foundation for trust.

</details>


### [253] [CoCo: Code as CoT for Text-to-Image Preview and Rare Concept Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08652)
*Haodong Li,Chunmei Qing,Huanyu Zhang,Dongzhi Jiang,Yihang Zou,Hongbo Peng,Dingming Li,Yuhong Dai,ZePeng Lin,Juanxi Tian,Yi Zhou,Siqi Dai,Jingwei Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCo（Code-as-CoT），一种利用可执行代码作为思维链推理的文本到图像生成框架，通过代码指定场景结构布局，在沙盒环境中渲染草图，再进行细粒度图像编辑优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于思维链的文本到图像方法依赖抽象的自然语言规划，缺乏处理复杂空间布局、结构化视觉元素和密集文本内容所需的精度。

Method: CoCo框架首先根据文本提示生成可执行代码以指定场景的结构布局，然后在沙盒环境中执行代码渲染确定性草图图像，最后通过细粒度图像编辑优化草图以生成最终高保真图像。构建了CoCo-10K数据集，包含结构化草图-最终图像对用于训练。

Result: 在StructT2IBench、OneIG-Bench和LongText-Bench上，CoCo相比直接生成分别提升68.83%、54.8%和41.23%，同时优于其他CoT增强的生成方法。

Conclusion: 可执行代码是一种有效且可靠的推理范式，能够实现精确、可控和结构化的文本到图像生成。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) have significantly advanced text-to-image (T2I) generation, particularly through the integration of Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning. However, existing CoT-based T2I methods largely rely on abstract natural-language planning, which lacks the precision required for complex spatial layouts, structured visual elements, and dense textual content. In this work, we propose CoCo (Code-as-CoT), a code-driven reasoning framework that represents the reasoning process as executable code, enabling explicit and verifiable intermediate planning for image generation. Given a text prompt, CoCo first generates executable code that specifies the structural layout of the scene, which is then executed in a sandboxed environment to render a deterministic draft image. The model subsequently refines this draft through fine-grained image editing to produce the final high-fidelity result. To support this training paradigm, we construct CoCo-10K, a curated dataset containing structured draft-final image pairs designed to teach both structured draft construction and corrective visual refinement. Empirical evaluations on StructT2IBench, OneIG-Bench, and LongText-Bench show that CoCo achieves improvements of +68.83%, +54.8%, and +41.23% over direct generation, while also outperforming other generation methods empowered by CoT. These results demonstrate that executable code is an effective and reliable reasoning paradigm for precise, controllable, and structured text-to-image generation. The code is available at: https://github.com/micky-li-hd/CoCo

</details>


### [254] [OfficeQA Pro: An Enterprise Benchmark for End-to-End Grounded Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08655)
*Krista Opsahl-Ong,Arnav Singhvi,Jasmine Collins,Ivan Zhou,Cindy Wang,Ashutosh Baheti,Owen Oertell,Jacob Portes,Sam Havens,Erich Elsen,Michael Bendersky,Matei Zaharia,Xing Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We introduce OfficeQA Pro, a benchmark for evaluating AI agents on grounded, multi-document reasoning over a large and heterogeneous document corpus. The corpus consists of U.S. Treasury Bulletins spanning nearly 100 years, comprising 89,000 pages and over 26 million numerical values. OfficeQA Pro consists of 133 questions that require precise document parsing, retrieval, and analytical reasoning across both unstructured text and tabular data. Frontier LLMs including Claude Opus 4.6, GPT-5.4, and Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview achieve less than 5% accuracy on OfficeQA Pro when relying on parametric knowledge, and less than 12% with additional access to the web. When provided directly with the document corpus, frontier agents still struggle on over half of questions, scoring 34.1% on average. We find that providing agents with a structured document representation produced by Databricks' ai_parse_document yields a 16.1% average relative performance gain across agents. We conduct additional ablations to study the effects of model selection, table representation, retrieval strategy, and test-time scaling on performance. Despite these improvements, significant headroom remains before agents can be considered reliable at enterprise-grade grounded reasoning.

</details>


### [255] [A Multi-Objective Optimization Approach for Sustainable AI-Driven Entrepreneurship in Resilient Economies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08692)
*Anas ALsobeh,Raneem Alkurdi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EcoAI-Resilience框架是一种多目标优化方法，旨在最大化AI部署的可持续性效益，同时最小化环境成本并增强经济韧性。该框架整合了53个国家、14个行业、2015-2024年间的能源消耗、可持续性指标、经济绩效和创业成果等多源数据，验证了R分数超过0.99的优异性能，识别出100%可再生能源整合、80%效率提升目标、202.48美元/人最优投资水平等关键策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI技术的快速发展为可持续经济发展带来前所未有的机遇和挑战。一方面，AI有潜力应对环境挑战并增强经济韧性；另一方面，AI部署通常涉及大量能源消耗和环境成本。因此需要一种平衡AI部署可持续性效益与环境成本的框架。

Method: 提出EcoAI-Resilience框架，采用多目标优化方法，通过数学优化解决三个关键目标：可持续性影响最大化、经济韧性增强和环境成本最小化。整合了来自53个国家、14个行业（2015-2024年）的多源数据，包括能源消耗指标、可持续性指标、经济绩效数据和创业成果。

Result: 实验验证显示所有模型组件R分数超过0.99，显著优于基线方法（线性回归R=0.943、随机森林R=0.957、梯度提升R=0.989）。框架成功识别出最优AI部署策略：100%可再生能源整合、80%效率改进目标、人均最优投资202.48美元。关键发现包括：经济复杂性与韧性呈强相关(r=0.82)、可再生能源采用与可持续性成果呈正相关(r=0.71)、全球AI准备度年均提升1.12分、可再生能源采用年均提升0.67年。

Conclusion: EcoAI-Resilience框架在AI驱动的可持续经济发展研究中具有重要贡献。通过多目标优化方法成功平衡了可持续性效益、经济韧性和环境成本，为政策制定者和企业提供了基于证据的AI部署策略指导。框架验证了AI与可持续发展目标兼容的可能性，强调了可再生能源整合和经济复杂性建设对增强AI部署效益的关键作用。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of artificial intelligence (AI) technologies presents both unprecedented opportunities and significant challenges for sustainable economic development. While AI offers transformative potential for addressing environmental challenges and enhancing economic resilience, its deployment often involves substantial energy consumption and environmental costs. This research introduces the EcoAI-Resilience framework, a multi-objective optimization approach designed to maximize the sustainability benefits of AI deployment while minimizing environmental costs and enhancing economic resilience. The framework addresses three critical objectives through mathematical optimization: sustainability impact maximization, economic resilience enhancement, and environmental cost minimization. The methodology integrates diverse data sources, including energy consumption metrics, sustainability indicators, economic performance data, and entrepreneurship outcomes across 53 countries and 14 sectors from 2015-2024. Our experimental validation demonstrates exceptional performance with R scores exceeding 0.99 across all model components, significantly outperforming baseline methods, including Linear Regression (R = 0.943), Random Forest (R = 0.957), and Gradient Boosting (R = 0.989). The framework successfully identifies optimal AI deployment strategies featuring 100\% renewable energy integration, 80% efficiency improvement targets, and optimal investment levels of $202.48 per capita. Key findings reveal strong correlations between economic complexity and resilience (r = 0.82), renewable energy adoption and sustainability outcomes (r = 0.71), and demonstrate significant temporal improvements in AI readiness (+1.12 points/year) and renewable energy adoption (+0.67 year) globally.

</details>


### [256] [Evaluating Financial Intelligence in Large Language Models: Benchmarking SuperInvesting AI with LLM Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08704)
*Akshay Gulati,Kanha Singhania,Tushar Banga,Parth Arora,Anshul Verma,Vaibhav Kumar Singh,Agyapal Digra,Jayant Singh Bisht,Danish Sharma,Varun Singla,Shubh Garg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI金融智能基准测试(AFIB)，一个用于评估大语言模型金融分析能力的多维框架，从事实准确性、分析完整性、数据时效性、模型一致性和失败模式五个维度评估了GPT、Gemini、Perplexity、Claude和SuperInvesting五款AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在金融分析和投资研究中的应用日益广泛，但缺乏系统性的金融推理能力评估标准。

Method: 构建了包含95+个结构化金融分析问题的数据集，来源于真实股票研究任务，从五个维度评估AI系统的金融分析能力。

Result: SuperInvesting获得最高综合表现，平均事实准确性得分8.96/10，分析完整性得分56.65/70，幻觉率最低。Perplexity在数据时效性任务上表现突出，但在分析综合和一致性方面较弱。

Conclusion: 金融智能具有内在多维特性，结合结构化金融数据访问与分析推理能力的系统能够为复杂投资研究工作流程提供最可靠的性能。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for financial analysis and investment research, yet systematic evaluation of their financial reasoning capabilities remains limited. In this work, we introduce the AI Financial Intelligence Benchmark (AFIB), a multi-dimensional evaluation framework designed to assess financial analysis capabilities across five dimensions: factual accuracy, analytical completeness, data recency, model consistency, and failure patterns. We evaluate five AI systems: GPT, Gemini, Perplexity, Claude, and SuperInvesting, using a dataset of 95+ structured financial analysis questions derived from real-world equity research tasks. The results reveal substantial differences in performance across models. Within this benchmark setting, SuperInvesting achieves the highest aggregate performance, with an average factual accuracy score of 8.96/10 and the highest completeness score of 56.65/70, while also demonstrating the lowest hallucination rate among evaluated systems. Retrieval-oriented systems such as Perplexity perform strongly on data recency tasks due to live information access but exhibit weaker analytical synthesis and consistency. Overall, the results highlight that financial intelligence in large language models is inherently multi-dimensional, and systems that combine structured financial data access with analytical reasoning capabilities provide the most reliable performance for complex investment research workflows.

</details>


### [257] [Agentic Critical Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08706)
*Weize Liu,Minghui Liu,Sy-Tuyen Ho,Souradip Chakraborty,Xiyao Wang,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ACT（智能体批判训练），通过强化学习训练智能体自主判断动作优劣而非模仿反思文本，在三个智能体基准上相比模仿学习、强化学习和知识蒸馏方法分别提升5.07、4.62和2.42分，并展现出良好的分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模仿学习仅教会智能体"做什么"而不理解"为什么"，智能体缺乏对动作质量的意识，因为它们从未对比成功与次优动作。现有的自我反思方法仍依赖于模仿学习（模仿构建好的反思文本），而非自主推理。

Method: 提出Agentic Critical Training (ACT)，一种强化学习范式，训练智能体在多个备选动作中识别更好的动作。通过奖励模型判断的正确性，促使模型自主发展关于动作质量的推理能力，产生真正的自我反思而非模仿反思文本。

Result: 在三个具有挑战性的智能体基准测试中，ACT结合不同后训练方法一致性地提升智能体性能。相较于模仿学习提升5.07分，相较于强化学习提升4.62分，相较于知识蒸馏方法提升2.42分。ACT还实现了智能体基准的强分布外泛化，并在无需推理特定训练数据的情况下提升通用推理基准的性能。

Conclusion: ACT是开发更具反思能力和更强智能体的一条有前景的路径。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) as autonomous agents often begins with imitation learning, but it only teaches agents what to do without understanding why: agents never contrast successful actions against suboptimal alternatives and thus lack awareness of action quality. Recent approaches attempt to address this by introducing self-reflection supervision derived from contrasts between expert and alternative actions. However, the training paradigm fundamentally remains imitation learning: the model imitates pre-constructed reflection text rather than learning to reason autonomously. We propose Agentic Critical Training (ACT), a reinforcement learning paradigm that trains agents to identify the better action among alternatives. By rewarding whether the model's judgment is correct, ACT drives the model to autonomously develop reasoning about action quality, producing genuine self-reflection rather than imitating it. Across three challenging agent benchmarks, ACT consistently improves agent performance when combined with different post-training methods. It achieves an average improvement of 5.07 points over imitation learning and 4.62 points over reinforcement learning. Compared to approaches that inject reflection capability through knowledge distillation, ACT also demonstrates clear advantages, yielding an average improvement of 2.42 points. Moreover, ACT enables strong out-of-distribution generalization on agentic benchmarks and improves performance on general reasoning benchmarks without any reasoning-specific training data, highlighting the value of our method. These results suggest that ACT is a promising path toward developing more reflective and capable LLM agents.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [258] [Configurable Runtime Orchestration for Dynamic Data Retrieval in Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06980)
*Abhiram Kandiraju*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种配置驱动的运行时编排框架，能够在请求时根据配置动态生成执行图，实现低延迟编排和独立任务的并行执行，解决传统工作流平台在集成演进时需要重新部署的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代企业平台依赖分布式微服务、分析数据平台和外部API来构建复合响应。跨异构系统编排数据检索具有挑战性，因为许多工作流平台依赖于预定义的工作流或状态机定义。Apache Airflow、AWS Step Functions和Temporal等系统虽功能强大，但通常假设工作流在执行前就已定义。

Method: 提出一种配置驱动的运行时编排框架，用于分布式系统中的动态数据检索。该框架在请求时根据配置动态生成执行图，使集成演进时无需重新部署工作流代码即可实现低延迟编排。执行规划器执行依赖感知的调度和独立任务的并行执行，实现跨分布式服务的高效聚合。

Result: 实现了无需重新部署工作流代码即可进行低延迟编排的能力，支持集成演进时的灵活性。允许跨分布式服务进行高效聚合。

Conclusion: 运行时配置可以实现在快速演进的集成环境中灵活且可扩展的编排。

Abstract: Modern enterprise platforms increasingly depend on distributed microservices, analytical data platforms, and external APIs to construct composite responses for applications. Orchestrating data retrieval across these heterogeneous systems is challenging because many workflow platforms rely on predefined workflows or state-machine definitions. Systems such as Apache Airflow, AWS Step Functions, and Temporal provide powerful orchestration capabilities but typically assume workflows are defined prior to execution. This paper presents a configuration-driven runtime orchestration framework for dynamic data retrieval in distributed systems. The framework generates execution graphs dynamically from configuration at request time, enabling low-latency orchestration without redeploying workflow code when integrations evolve. The execution planner performs dependency-aware scheduling and parallel execution of independent tasks, allowing efficient aggregation across distributed services. The paper describes the architecture, execution model, and operational tradeoffs of this framework, and presents a representative enterprise case study for Customer 360 retrieval. The approach demonstrates how runtime configuration can enable flexible and scalable orchestration in rapidly evolving integration environments.

</details>


### [259] [AIReSim: A Discrete Event Simulator for Large-scale AI Cluster Reliability Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07041)
*Karthik Pattabiraman,Mihir Patel,Fred Lin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AIReSim，一个用于评估大型AI集群故障恢复、调度和修复机制的离散事件仿真器，旨在帮助系统设计者优化各种参数和旋钮，以提升系统端到端可靠性并支持容量规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型AI集群中运行大规模AI工作负载时故障成本高（需从检查点重启整个作业），导致集群利用率下降。虽然已有多种故障缓解机制，但这些机制涉及大量参数和旋钮（knobs），需要根据系统和集群特性进行仔细调优。现有方法缺乏系统性的评估工具来优化这些参数。

Method: 构建了AIReSim，一个离散事件仿真器，用于评估大型AI工作负载集群在故障、恢复、调度和修复过程中的不同设计选择。AIReSim允许系统设计者系统性地评估不同旋钮和参数对系统端到端可靠性的影响，识别重要参数以优先改进系统，并支持调优以实现不同权衡及分析各种"假设"场景。

Result: 提供了将AIReSim应用于大规模AI工作负载集群容量规划的案例研究。

Conclusion: AIReSim是一个有效的工具，可用于系统性评估大型AI集群中故障恢复和调度机制的设计选择，支持容量规划和参数优化。

Abstract: Failures in clusters running large-scale AI workloads can result in decreased utilization. Because the cost of a failure in such AI workloads is high (as it requires restarting the entire job from a previous checkpoint), there are many mechanisms in place to ensure that the failures are mitigated, and the impact of a failure is minimized. However, these mechanisms have many knobs and parameters, all of which must be carefully tuned based on the system and cluster's characteristics. We built AIReSim, a discrete event simulator to evaluate the different design choices during the failure, recovery, scheduling and repair processes for a cluster running a large-scale AI workload.
  AIReSim allows the system designer to systematically evaluate the effects of the different knobs and parameters on the overall end-to-end reliability of the system. Further, AIReSim can be used to identify which knobs or parameters are important in order to prioritize the investment of effort in improving the system. AIReSim also allows tuning of the knobs for achieving different tradeoffs in the system, as well as to consider various ``what-if'' scenarios. We present a case study of applying AIReSim for capacity planning for large-scale clusters running AI workloads.

</details>


### [260] [Uber's Failover Architecture: Reconciling Reliability and Efficiency in Hyperscale Microservice Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07345)
*Mayank Bansal,Milind Chabbi,Kenneth Bogh,Srikanth Prodduturi,Kevin Xu,Amit Kumar,David Bell,Ranjib Dey,Yufei Ren,Sachin Sharma,Juan Marcano,Shriniket Kale,Subhav Pradhan,Ivan Beschastnikh,Miguel Covarrubias,Chien-Chih Liao,Sandeep Koushik Sheshadri,Wen Luo,Kai Song,Ashish Samant,Sahil Rihan,Nimish Sheth,Uday Kiran Medisetty*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Uber的故障转移架构(UFA)通过业务关键性分级策略，将传统2x容量模型优化为1.3x配置，在保持99.97%可用性的同时将利用率从20%提升至30%，并削减了超过100万CPU核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Uber原有的基础设施采用2x容量模型（每个服务在全球两个区域独立配置以处理全球流量），导致一半的fleet闲置，资源利用率仅约20%，造成严重的资源浪费和成本问题。

Method: UFA采用以下核心技术方法：(1)按业务关键性分级架构，关键服务保留故障转移保障，非关键服务在稳态下可临时使用关键服务的故障转移缓冲容量；(2)"全峰值"故障转移时选择性抢占非关键服务，使用按需容量满足差异化SLA；(3)自动化保护机制，包括依赖关系分析和回归门禁，确保关键服务在非关键服务不可用时仍能正常运行。

Result: UFA将稳态配置从2x降低至1.3x，利用率从~20%提升至~30%，同时维持99.97%的可用性。已加固超过4000个不安全依赖项，从约400万核心的基线中削减了超过100万CPU核心。

Conclusion: UFA成功用差异化架构替代了传统的2x容量模型，在保持99.97%可用性的同时大幅提升了资源利用效率。该架构通过业务关键性分级、自动保护机制和弹性容量策略，实现了成本与可靠性的平衡。

Abstract: Operating a global, real-time platform at Uber's scale requires infrastructure that is both resilient and cost-efficient. Historically, reliability was ensured through a costly 2x capacity model--each service provisioned to handle global traffic independently across two regions--leaving half the fleet idle. We present Uber's Failover Architecture (UFA), which replaces the uniform 2x model with a differentiated architecture aligned to business criticality. Critical services retain failover guarantees, while non-critical services opportunistically use failover buffer capacity reserved for critical services during steady state. During rare "full-peak" failovers, non-critical services are selectively preempted and rapidly restored, with differentiated Service-Level Agreements (SLAs) using on-demand capacity. Automated safeguards, including dependency analysis and regression gates, ensure critical services continue to function even while non-critical services are unavailable. The quantitative impact is significant: UFA reduces steady-state provisioning from 2x to 1.3x, raising utilization from ~20% to ~30% while sustaining 99.97% availability. To date, UFA has hardened over 4,000 unsafe dependencies, eliminated over one million CPU cores from a baseline of about four million cores.

</details>


### [261] [MAS-H2: A Hierarchical Multi-Agent System for Holistic Cloud-Native Autoscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07607)
*Hamed Hamzeh,Parisa Vahdatian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MAS-H2是一个分层多智能体系统，解决Kubernetes中自动扩缩容的战略空缺问题。系统包含三层：战略智能体（将业务策略转化为全局效用函数）、规划智能体（基于时间序列预测生成pod和节点联合扩展计划）、执行智能体（执行扩展计划）。在GKE上实现并测试，相比原生HPA在两种工作负载场景下显著降低CPU负载并实现零停机战略迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云原生平台（如Kubernetes）的自动扩缩容是反应式和指标驱动的，导致战略空缺问题。业务策略与底层资源调配脱节，加上pod和节点扩展的碎片化协调，在动态工作负载下会导致严重的资源浪费和性能下降。

Method: 提出MAS-H2分层多智能体系统，将控制问题系统分解为三层：1) 战略智能体将业务策略（如成本vs性能）形式化为全局效用函数；2) 规划智能体通过时间序列预测生成pod和节点的联合主动扩展计划；3) 执行智能体执行扩展计划。作为Kubernetes Operator在Google Kubernetes Engine (GKE)上实现原型。

Result: 在两个真实、突发和高压力工作负载场景下测试：1) 可预测的Heartbeat工作负载：MAS-H2将应用CPU使用率维持在40%以下，比通常运行在80%以上的原生HPA减少超过50%的持续CPU压力；2) 混沌闪购场景：展示主动规划能力，过滤瞬时噪声并部署更多副本，在不欠配置的情况下将峰值CPU负载降低55%。此外，MAS-H2在两种成本和性能优化的基础设施之间实现了零停机战略迁移。

Conclusion: MAS-H2通过分层多智能体架构成功解决了云资源管理中的战略空缺问题，实现了主动式资源规划、显著的CPU负载降低以及零停机基础设施迁移，为云原生环境中的自主资源管理提供了完整的端到端解决方案。

Abstract: Autoscaling in cloud-native platforms like Kubernetes is reactive and metric-driven, leading to a strategic void problem. This comes from the decoupling of higher-level business policies from lower-level resource provisioning. The strategic void, coupled with a fragmented coordination of pod and node scaling, can lead to significant resource waste and performance degradation under dynamic workloads. In this paper, we present MAS-H2, a new hierarchical multi-agent system that addresses the challenges of autonomic cloud resource management with a complete end-to-end solution. MAS-H2 systematically decomposes the control problem into three layers: a Strategic Agent that formalises business policies (e.g., cost vs. performance) into a global utility function; Planning Agents that produce a joint, proactive scaling plan for pods and nodes with time-series forecasting; and Execution Agents that execute the scaling plan. We built and tested a MAS-H2 prototype as a Kubernetes Operator on Google Kubernetes Engine (GKE) to benchmark it against the native Horizontal Pod Autoscaler (HPA) and Cluster Autoscaler (CA) baselines under two realistic, spiky, and stress-inducing workload scenarios. The results show that the MAS-H2 system maintained application CPU usage under 40% for predictable Heartbeat workloads. This resulted in over 50% less sustained CPU stress than the native HPA baseline, which typically operated above 80%. The MAS-H2 system demonstrated proactive planning in a volatile Chaotic Flash Sale scenario by filtering transient noise and deploying more replicas compared to HPA. It reduced peak CPU load by 55% without under-provisioning. Beyond performance, MAS-H2 seamlessly performed a zero-downtime strategic migration between two cost- and performance-optimised infrastructures.

</details>


### [262] [Performance Evaluation of Automated Multi-Service Deployment in Edge-Cloud Environments with the CODECO Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07621)
*Georgios Koukis,Ioannis Dermentzis,Vassilis Tsaoussidis,Jan Lenke,Fabian Wolk,Daniel Uceda,Guillermo Sanchez,Miguel A. Puentes,Javier Serrano,Panagiotis Karamolegkos,Rute C. Sofia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了CODECO工具包，这是一个用于增强分布式基础设施容器编排的开源框架。通过在多种硬件平台(ARM、AMD、RPi)和K8s分发版(包括轻量级k3s)上与基线K8s工作流进行对比实验，验证了CODECO在减少手动工作量的同时保持竞争性能的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构边缘-云环境中，自动化的多服务应用部署和管理仍具挑战性。Kubernetes(K8s)作为主导的编排平台，需要增强其对分布式基础设施的支持能力。

Method: 使用三个关键性能指标评估CODECO：部署时间、手动干预程度、以及运行时性能与资源利用率。实验覆盖多种硬件平台(ARM、AMD、RPi)和K8s分发版(包括轻量级k3s)。

Result: 实验结果表明，CODECO显著减少了手动工作量，同时保持了竞争性的性能和可接受的开销。

Conclusion: CODECO是边缘-云编排的有效解决方案，能够提高基于K8s部署的灵活性和智能化程度。

Abstract: Containerized microservices are widely adopted for latency-sensitive and compute-intensive applications, with Kubernetes (K8s) as the dominant orchestration platform. However, automating the deployment and management of multi-service applications remains challenging, particularly in heterogeneous Edge-Cloud environments. This paper evaluates the CODECO toolkit, an open-source framework designed to enhance container orchestration across distributed infrastructures. We compare CODECO with baseline K8s workflows using three key performance indicators: deployment time, level of manual intervention, and runtime performance with resource utilization. Experiments across diverse hardware platforms (ARM, AMD, RPi) and K8s distributions, including lightweight variants such as k3s, demonstrate that CODECO substantially reduces manual effort while maintaining competitive performance and acceptable overhead. These results validate CODECO as an effective solution for Edge-Cloud orchestration and highlight its potential to improve the flexibility and intelligence of K8s-based deployments.

</details>


### [263] [Scalable Training of Mixture-of-Experts Models with Megatron Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07685)
*Zijie Yan,Hongxiao Bai,Xin Yao,Dennis Liu,Tong Liu,Hongbin Liu,Pingtian Li,Evan Wu,Shiqing Fan,Li Tao,Robin Zhang,Yuzhong Wang,Shifang Xu,Jack Chang,Xuwen Chen,Kunlun Li,Yan Bai,Gao Deng,Nan Zheng,Vijay Anand Korthikanti,Abhinav Khattar,Ethan He,Soham Govande,Sangkug Lym,Zhongbo Zhu,Qi Zhang,Haochen Yuan,Xiaowei Ren,Deyu Fu,Tailai Ma,Shunkang Zhang,Jiang Shao,Ray Wang,Santosh Bhavani,Xipeng Li,Chandler Zhou,David Wu,Yingcan Wei,Ashwath Aithal,Michael Andersch,Mohammad Shoeybi,Jiajie Yao,June Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对MoE（混合专家）模型训练的综合系统优化框架，通过跨内存、通信和计算维度的协同优化，解决了稀疏MoE模型扩展中的系统挑战，实现了高达1,233 TFLOPS/GPU的训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型的稀疏特性允许总参数量快速增长，但每个token只激活部分专家，导致内存、通信和计算之间形成耦合约束。优化某一维度通常会将压力转移到另一维度，需要跨整个系统栈的协同设计。

Method: 集成优化涵盖：1) 内存优化：细粒度重计算、卸载策略；2) 通信优化：优化的调度器、通信与计算重叠；3) 计算优化：分组GEMM、算子融合、CUDA Graphs；4) 并行折叠实现灵活多维并行；5) 低精度训练支持FP8和NVFP4；6) 长上下文高效训练。

Result: 在NVIDIA GB300和GB200上，DeepSeek-V3-685B达到1,233/1,048 TFLOPS/GPU，Qwen3-235B达到974/919 TFLOPS/GPU。该框架已在学术和工业界用于训练数十亿到万亿参数的MoE模型，扩展到数千GPU集群。

Conclusion: 该框架作为高性能、可扩展的生产级开源解决方案，基于Megatron Core提供了MoE模型扩展的实用指导，揭示了各优化技术在系统层面的权衡和相互作用。

Abstract: Scaling Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) training introduces systems challenges absent in dense models. Because each token activates only a subset of experts, this sparsity allows total parameters to grow much faster than per-token computation, creating coupled constraints across memory, communication, and computation. Optimizing one dimension often shifts pressure to another, demanding co-design across the full system stack.
  We address these challenges for MoE training through integrated optimizations spanning memory (fine-grained recomputation, offloading, etc.), communication (optimized dispatchers, overlapping, etc.), and computation (Grouped GEMM, fusions, CUDA Graphs, etc.). The framework also provides Parallel Folding for flexible multi-dimensional parallelism, low-precision training support for FP8 and NVFP4, and efficient long-context training. On NVIDIA GB300 and GB200, it achieves 1,233/1,048 TFLOPS/GPU for DeepSeek-V3-685B and 974/919 TFLOPS/GPU for Qwen3-235B. As a performant, scalable, and production-ready open-source solution, it has been used across academia and industry for training MoE models ranging from billions to trillions of parameters on clusters scaling up to thousands of GPUs.
  This report explains how these techniques work, their trade-offs, and their interactions at the systems level, providing practical guidance for scaling MoE models with Megatron Core.

</details>


### [264] [The Consistency Correctness in CoPPar Tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07884)
*Xincheng Yang,Kyle Hale*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文档是CoPPar Tree论文的补充材料，通过形式化方法证明了CoPPar架构的正确性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为CoPPar Tree论文提供理论支撑，验证CoPPar架构的正确性

Method: 通过形式化方法进行详细正确性证明

Result: 完成CoPPar架构正确性的形式化验证

Conclusion: 提供了CoPPar架构的详细正确性证明，补充验证了原始CoPPar Tree论文的理论基础

Abstract: This article is a supplementary document for the CoPPar Tree paper, providing a detailed correctness proof for the CoPPar architecture.

</details>


### [265] [SageSched: Efficient LLM Scheduling Confronting Demand Uncertainty and Hybridity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07917)
*Zhenghao Gan,Yichen Bao,Yifei Liu,Chen Chen,Quan Chen,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SageSched，一个针对大语言模型推理的高效调度器，通过预测输出长度分布和建模计算+内存服务成本，结合不确定性感知调度策略，实现28.7%的效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理具有需求不确定性（输出长度事先未知）和混合性（既计算密集又内存密集）的特点，现有调度器依赖简单启发式方法或仅关注计算资源，性能表现不佳。

Method: SageSched结合提示内容和历史推理结果预测输出长度分布；建模考虑计算和内存的真正服务成本；采用不确定性感知的调度策略优化整体效率。

Result: 测试实验证实SageSched可实现超过28.7%的效率提升。

Conclusion: 通过有效处理需求不确定性和混合性，SageSched为LLM推理调度提供了一种高效解决方案，显著提升了系统整体性能。

Abstract: Efficient LLM inference scheduling is crucial for user experience.However, LLM inferences exhibit remarkable demand uncertainty (with unknown output length beforehand) and hybridity (being both compute and memory intensive). Existing LLM schedulers rely on simple heuristics or focus purely on compute resource, suffering suboptimal performance.
  In this work, we propose SageSched, an efficient LLM scheduler that properly handles demand uncertainty and hybridity of inference workloads.SageSched combines prompt contents with the past inference results to predict output-length distribution in a light-weight and also accurate manner.Meanwhile, it models the true service cost of an inference request with both compute and memory aspects considered.Finally, SageSched employs an uncertainty-aware scheduling policy that can yield the best overall efficiency given the request cost distributions.Testbed experiments over diverse setups confirm that SageSched can attain an efficiency improvement of over 28.7%.

</details>


### [266] [RAPID: Redundancy-Aware and Compatibility-Optimal Edge-Cloud Partitioned Inference for Diverse VLA models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07949)
*Zihao Zheng,Sicheng Tian,Hangyu Cao,Chenyue Li,Jiayu Chen,Maoliang Li,Xinhao Sun,Hailong Zou,Guojie Luo,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对VLA模型推理成本高的问题，提出了一种名为RAPID的边云协同推理框架，通过解决视觉噪声干扰和步骤级冗余问题，实现了1.73倍的加速，仅有5%~7%的开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在具身智能中广泛应用，但面临高推理成本的问题。现有边云协同框架存在两个关键挑战：(1) 主流环境导向的边云分割方法容易受到视觉噪声干扰；(2) 现有方法忽略了具身任务中独特的步骤级冗余，影响运动的物理连续性。

Method: 提出了一种新型的边云协同推理框架RAPID，并开发了针对该框架的具体实现方案。该框架专门针对VLA模型设计，解决了视觉噪声干扰和步骤级冗余问题。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法实现了最高1.73倍的加速效果，同时仅产生5%~7%的开销。

Conclusion: RAPID框架能够有效降低VLA模型的推理成本，在保持较低开销的前提下显著提升推理速度，为边云协同推理在具身智能领域的应用提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision Language Action (VLA) models are mainstream in embodied intelligence but face high inference costs. Edge-Cloud Collaborative (ECC) inference offers an effective fix by easing edge-device computing pressure to meet real-time needs. However, existing ECC frameworks are suboptimal for VLA models due to two challenges: (1) Mainstream environment-oriented edge-cloud partitioning methods are susceptible to interference from visual noise; (2) Existing edge-cloud partitioning methods overlook the step-wise redundancy unique to embodied tasks, thereby disrupting the physical continuity of motion. To address these issues, we propose a novel ECC inference framework, termed RAPID. Specifically, we developed an implementation tailored to the proposed framework. Experiments demonstrate this achieves a speedup of up to 1.73x with only 5%~7% overhead.

</details>


### [267] [SI-ChainFL: Shapley-Incentivized Secure Federated Learning for High-Speed Rail Data Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07992)
*Mingjie Zhao,Cheng Dai,Fei Chen,Xin Chen,Kaoru Ota,Mianxiong Dong,Bing Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SI-ChainFL框架，解决HSR系统中联邦学习的激励不足和中心化聚合问题。该框架结合贡献感知激励和去中心化聚合，通过Shapley值量化客户端贡献，并设计区块链共识协议实现安全高效的去中心化聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HSR系统中联邦学习方案存在两个关键局限：(1) 激励不足，导致搭便车和模型投毒问题；(2) 中心化聚合引入单点故障风险。

Method: 提出SI-ChainFL框架，包含三个核心设计：1) 基于Shapley值的贡献量化指标，综合考虑稀有事件效用、数据多样性、数据质量和时效性；2) 稀有正样本驱动的客户端聚类策略，加速Shapley值估计；3) 基于区块链的共识协议进行去中心化聚合，将聚合资格与Shapley激励绑定。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和HSR流量数据集上的实验表明，SI-ChainFL在90%恶意客户端的PA攻击下仍能保持有效运行，精度比RAGA高14.12%。理论分析进一步保证了性能上界。

Conclusion: SI-ChainFL通过贡献感知激励和去中心化聚合有效解决了联邦学习中的激励不足和单点故障问题，在高恶意环境下仍能保持较好的准确性和安全性。

Abstract: In high-speed rail (HSR) systems, federated learning (FL) enables cross-departmental flow prediction without sharing raw data. However, existing schemes suffer from two key limitations: (1) insufficient incentives, leading to free-riding and model poisoning; and (2) centralized aggregation, which introduces a single point of failure. We propose a secure and efficient framework SI-ChainFL that addresses these issues by combining contribution-aware incentives with decentralized aggregation. First, we quantify client contributions using a Shapley value metric that jointly considers rare-event utility, data diversity, data quality, and timeliness. To reduce computational overhead, we further develop a rare positive driven client clustering strategy to accelerate Shapley estimation. Moreover, we design a blockchain-based consensus protocol for decentralized aggregation, where aggregation eligibility is tied to Shapley incentives. This design motivates clients to submit high-quality updates and enables efficient and secure global aggregation. Experiments on MNIST, CIFAR 10 and CIFAR 100, and a HSR flow dataset show that SI ChainFL remains effective under 90% malicious clients in PA attacks, achieving 14.12% higher accuracy than RAGA. Theoretical analysis further guarantees an upper bound on performance

</details>


### [268] [Covenant-72B: Pre-Training a 72B LLM with Trustless Peers Over-the-Internet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08163)
*Joel Lidin,Amir Sarfi,Erfan Miahi,Quentin Anthony,Shivam Chauhan,Evangelos Pappas,Benjamin Thérien,Eugene Belilovsky,Samuel Dare*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Covenant-72B，这是迄今为止规模最大的全球分布式预训练项目（72B参数，1.1T tokens），通过SparseLoCo优化器和区块链协议实现了完全开放、无许可的民主化参与，证明了大规模分布式训练的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全球分布式训练的模型规模较小且仅限白名单参与者参与，未能实现民主化参与的真正承诺。需要探索更大规模的民主化参与训练模式。

Method: 使用SparseLoCo（先进的通信高效优化器）支持动态参与，结合实时区块链协议实现开放的、无许可的参与机制。模型在约1.1T tokens上进行预训练。

Result: Covenant-72B在约1.1T tokens上预训练完成，在相同或更高计算预算下与完全中心化模型表现相当，是迄今为止规模最大的全球分布式预训练运行（按计算量和模型规模衡量）。

Conclusion: Covenant-72B证明了完全民主化、无白名单的全球分布式预训练在规模上是可以实现的。该模型在相同或更高计算预算下与完全中心化模型具有竞争力，验证了开放参与模式的可行性。

Abstract: Recently, there has been increased interest in globally distributed training, which has the promise to both reduce training costs and democratize participation in building large-scale foundation models. However, existing models trained in a globally distributed manner are relatively small in scale and have only been trained with whitelisted participants. Therefore, they do not yet realize the full promise of democratized participation. In this report, we describe Covenant-72B, an LLM produced by the largest collaborative globally distributed pre-training run (in terms of both compute and model scale), which simultaneously allowed open, permissionless participation supported by a live blockchain protocol. We utilized a state-of-the-art communication-efficient optimizer, SparseLoCo, supporting dynamic participation with peers joining and leaving freely. Our model, pre-trained on approximately 1.1T tokens, performs competitively with fully centralized models pre-trained on similar or higher compute budgets, demonstrating that fully democratized, non-whitelisted participation is not only feasible, but can be achieved at unprecedented scale for a globally distributed pre-training run.

</details>


### [269] [A Hodge-Based Framework for Service Operational Analysis in Serverless Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08192)
*Gianluca Reali,Mauro Femminella*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于拓扑模型和Hodge分解的serverless平台服务分析方法，用于分析无服务器架构中函数间的信息流，将操作流分解为局部可校正分量和全局持久谐波模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: serverless平台中的服务通常由编排的函数组成，由于独立部署的函数在粗粒度控制机制下的交互，会表现出复杂且非保守的信息流，需要系统性的分析方法。

Method: 引入serverless服务的拓扑模型，利用Hodge分解将观察到的操作流分解为局部可校正分量和全局持久的谐波模式。

Result: 谐波流自然地产生于函数间的不同类型交互，应被视为serverless系统的结构特性而非配置错误。实验证实该方法可揭示导致低效的潜在架构结构。

Conclusion: 提出了一种分析函数间流的系统方法论，包括推导可操作的补救策略（如阻尼效应）作为重构服务拓扑的替代方案，可包含谐波低效的影响。

Abstract: In this paper we propose a method for analyzing services deployed in serverless platforms. These services typically consists of orchestrated functions that can exhibit complex and non-conservative information flows due to the interaction of independently deployed functions under coarse-grained control mechanisms. We introduce a topological model of serverless services and make use of the Hodge decomposition to partition observed operational flows into locally correctable components and globally persistent harmonic modes. Our analysis shows that harmonic flows naturally arise from different kind of interactions among functions and should be interpreted as structural properties of serverless systems rather than configuration errors. We present a systematic methodology for analyzing inter-function flows and deriving actionable remediation strategies, including dumping effects to contain the effects of harmonic inefficiencies as an alternative to completely restructure the topological model of the service. Experimental results confirm that the proposed approach can uncover latent architectural structures leading to inefficiencies.

</details>
